([SakkoulasGiannis/addsynapse-woocommerce](https://github.com/SakkoulasGiannis/addsynapse-woocommerce/tree/HEAD/skills/addsynapse-woocommerce) · ★1)
- **addtests**: Add tests that cover core concepts and prevent regressions without overfitting or crystallizing the code base. Use when the user asks for tests, more coverage, or a regression test for a bug fix, or invokes `/z:addtests`. ([Zenable-io/ai-guardrails](https://github.com/Zenable-io/ai-guardrails/tree/HEAD/plugins/z/skills/addtests) · ★2)
- **addtests**: Write small pytest tests for Python code that has none, one use case at a time, entry points first, so the project has use cases to trace and explain. Use when a project has no tests or a part is untested. ([vishalbanwari26/flowscope](https://github.com/vishalbanwari26/flowscope/tree/HEAD/skills/addtests))
- **addtocartfrombitable**: 从飞书Bitable表格获取商品信息（链接、规格、数量），然后通过浏览器自动化将其加入天猫/淘宝购物车。触发词：加购物车、Bitable商品、批量加购、采购表格。 ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/addtocartfrombitable) · ★21 · 6 copies)
- **addtodo**: Add a quick to-do item to the project's TODO.md (the repo-root TODO.md in the current working directory), auto-routed to the most relevant existing section (or a new subsection if nothing fits). The item is whatever the user types after /addtodo. Use when the user says "/addtodo &lt;text&gt;", "addtodo", or asks to drop a new task or idea into TODO.md. ONLY for a repo whose tracker is a hand-edited TODO.md: if the repo has an items/ store (items/*.md with items/scripts/itemlib.py), stop and use /additem instead, even when a TODO.md also exists — a stale hand-authored TODO.md often sits alongside a… ([pianonik/gists](https://github.com/pianonik/gists/tree/HEAD/project-docs-plugin/skills/addtodo))
- **adduct-abundance-quantification-per-dataset**: Use when you have a peak table exported from MALDI-MSI software (SCiLS/MSiReader CSV or Cardinal peakBinned imzML) and want to estimate what fraction of peaks are adducts rather than true metabolite signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-abundance-quantification-per-dataset))
- **adduct-and-fragment-neutral-mass-calculation**: Use when you have an LC-MS peak-intensity matrix with observed m/z values (from negative or positive mode ionization) and need to map each peak to candidate neutral masses in KEGG. Use this skill when you have a curated Cpd. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-and-fragment-neutral-mass-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-and-in-source-fragment-filtering**: Use when assigning preannotated metabolite candidates (from a reaction-based pseudo-MS1 candidature space) to parent pesticides via an MS2 similarity network, and adduct/ISF-derived spectral artifacts must be filtered before accepting seed-molecule annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-and-in-source-fragment-filtering))
- **adduct-and-instrument-type-onehot-encoding**: Use when preparing the Mol2CCS feature matrix (parquet) from METLIN-CCS or CCSBase records: after computing 256-bit Morgan fingerprints, molecular weight, and 35-dimensional Drug Tax counts, encode adduct, molecule type, instrument type as one-hot vectors and the monomer/dimer association as a. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-and-instrument-type-onehot-encoding))
- **adduct-and-isotope-expanded-database-querying**: Use when you have a positive- or negative-mode m/z peaklist (e.g. MetaboLights MTBLS28-style lung cancer urine data) and need putative MSI level 2 identities including which adduct/isotope explains each m/z. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-and-isotope-expanded-database-querying))
- **adduct-and-lipid-class-search-space-definition**: Use when building a theoretical lipid m/z library for accurate-mass annotation of deisotoped MSI features and you must choose which lipid classes and adducts to enumerate for the given ionisation mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-and-lipid-class-search-space-definition))
- **adduct-annotation**: Use when converting raw LC-MS/MS mzML files into a processed peak table and each detected feature needs a putative adduct annotation before downstream filtering, normalization, or annotation with metID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation))
- **adduct-annotation-against-reference-library**: Use when after computing a histogram of all pairwise mass differences from an MSI dataset, use this skill when you have observed mass difference peaks that may correspond to known adducts (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M−H2O]+). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-annotation-against-reference-library) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-annotation-against-reference-table**: Use when you have a mass-difference histogram (or pairwise mass differences) from MS peaks and need to label which differences correspond to known metabolite–matrix or salt adducts before spatial correlation or peak filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation-against-reference-table))
- **adduct-annotation-coherence-checking**: Use when curating a reference MS/MS library (e.g. MGF-format spectra with SMILES, adduct, ionization mode, and precursor m/z metadata) where chemically incoherent annotations must be detected and removed before library cleaning or machine-learning model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation-coherence-checking))
- **adduct-annotation-feature-linking**: Use when a targeted re-quantification (FeatureFinderMetaboIdent) has replaced intensity values in the feature matrix and the resulting consensus map needs adduct re-annotation, ID re-mapping and re-linking across all samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation-feature-linking))
- **adduct-annotation-lcms-features**: Use when you have an MZmine3 feature table (or equivalent feature list with representative MS/MS spectra) from positive ion mode LC–MS/MS data and need per-feature molecular formula and adduct assignments before candidate structure retrieval. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation-lcms-features))
- **adduct-annotation-library-construction**: Use when you have authentic metabolite standards run in positive and negative ESI mode as mzML files and need to build a within-laboratory RT/m/z library without relying on MS/MS spectral data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation-library-construction))
- **adduct-annotation-of-aligned-peaklists**: Use when processing MS1-only untargeted LC/HRMS data (mzXML, mzML, netCDF) with IDSL.CSA and the aligned peak table needs potential adduct information attached to grouped correlating ions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation-of-aligned-peaklists))
- **adduct-annotation-tallying**: Decision support for quantifying ion-species bias in LC-MS2 positive-mode spectral libraries (e.g. MassBank of North America, GNPS) by tallying adduct annotations such as [M+H]+ versus less common ion types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation-tallying))
- **adduct-annotation-via-metadata-matching**: Use when building an MSn spectral library from Orbitrap ID-X acquisitions and each file must be linked to its compound identity (adduct, in-source fragment) via a metadata table keyed on a unique sample ID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation-via-metadata-matching))
- **adduct-assembly-combinatorics**: Use when building targeted lipidomics precursor/fragment transition lists from lipid class definitions and adduct rules, or when auditing how many lipid classes/molecules an in-silico lipid knowledge base covers. - **adduct-assignment-accuracy-assessment**: Use when you have a trained formula ranking model (such as MIST-CF) and want to measure the specific performance gain from incorporating multiple positive-mode adduct types (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+K]+, [M+NH4]+) instead of restricting predictions to [M+H]+ only. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-assignment-accuracy-assessment) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-assignment-mass-spectrometry**: Use when a set of tumor-correlated m/z peaks (Pearson correlation ≥ 0.7 with the tumor-class prediction map) from MALDI FT-ICR MSI data needs tentative molecular formula and adduct annotation before downstream interpretation or table export. - **adduct-aware-ccs-prediction-evaluation**: Use when you need to test whether a CCS prediction model trained on one ion-mobility database (e.g. CCSBase v1.3, 16,989 values / 6,744 molecules) generalizes to a structurally different database such as METLIN-CCS (&gt;27,000 unique synthetic small-molecule structures). - **adduct-aware-database-search**: Use when an imzML/mzML-derived m/z peak list from spatial metabolomics data (especially MALDI-TOF, low/medium resolution) needs metabolite annotation against HMDB or LipidMaps in a defined adduct/polarity space, and METASPACE/pySM FDR-based annotation is not applicable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-aware-database-search))
- **adduct-aware-feature-annotation**: Use when annotating LC–MS untargeted metabolomics features against a compound database in either positive or negative ionisation mode, and candidate metabolites must be scored accounting for the specific adducts they can form. - **adduct-aware-ion-annotation**: Use when assigning metabolite identities to MS1 m/z features by querying metabolomic databases such as HMDB, and candidates must be filtered and ranked according to ionization mode, adduct knowledge, and compound properties. - **adduct-aware-ion-species-matching**: Use when aligning multiple processed LC-HRMS target feature lists against a reference feature list where the same metabolite appears as different ion adducts, and naive m/z-only matching undercounts matched features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-aware-ion-species-matching))
- **adduct-aware-lipid-library-querying**: Use when starting lipid annotation from an MZmine/MS-DIAL/XCMS feature table (m/z, RT, intensities) and you need candidate lipid species/adducts matched by precursor exact mass before any MS/MS fragment matching. - **adduct-based-annotation-prioritization**: Use when a list of m/z-to-compound matches (with adduct, isotope label, ppm error, and source database columns) from a MetaboShiny/MetaDBparse search must be prioritized into putative (MSI level 2) annotations. - **adduct-based-feature-consolidation**: Use when after accurate mass searching has assigned multiple detected m/z features to the same metabolite via positive and negative adduct libraries, and before sample-level feature merging. - **adduct-ion-mass-matching**: Use when you have unlabeled LC/MS feature tables (m/z + retention time per feature) and need to estimate what fraction of features correspond to known metabolites via negative- or positive-mode adduct matching, e.g. to benchmark preprocessing methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-pair-identification))
- **adduct-ion-parent-ion-pairing-analysis**: Use when when you have binned mass spectrometry imaging peaks and want to understand which detected mass-to-charge ratios represent the same metabolite in different ionization states (parent vs. adduct form). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-ion-parent-ion-pairing-analysis) · 4 copies)
- **adduct-ion-prediction-and-filtering**: Use when when annotating m/z features against a metabolite database (HMDB, Lipidmaps, etc.) and the sample preparation, ionization method, or polarity mode favors specific adduct species. For example: negative-mode LC-MS or MS imaging will preferentially generate M-H and halide adducts (M+Cl); ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-ion-prediction-and-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-ion-species-prediction**: Use when you have a list of molecular formulae for LC-IM-MS / drift tube IM-MS data and need predicted ion species (primary ion plus adducts, positive or negative mode, multiply charged) to build targeted m/z search traces. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-species-prediction))
- **adduct-ion-targeted-search-in-rt-window**: Use when the primary ion of a molecular formula has a confirmed RT window in a profile LC-DTIM-MS mzML file and you need to screen additional adduct ions (positive/negative mode), extract IM peaks, and derive DTCCS(N2) values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-targeted-search-in-rt-window))
- **adduct-ion-variant-matching**: Use when validating a molecular network's ability to recover adduct-related spectral pairs — specifically sodiated variants disconnected from protonated precursors under similarity-threshold networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-variant-matching))
- **adduct-ionmode-consistency-checking**: Use when parsing, standardizing, or filtering MS spectra from mixed or heterogeneous databases where adduct assignment may be manually entered, auto-inferred, or missing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-ionmode-consistency-checking) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-ionmode-cross-validation**: Use when cleaning an MS/MS spectral library where spectra carry adduct, ionmode, precursor m/z and parent mass metadata (e.g. .msp/.MGF GNPS entries) and you must detect inconsistent annotations before matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ionmode-cross-validation))
- **adduct-isotope-analysis**: Analyze adduct and isotope evidence for one m/z or molecule — multi-adduct groups, isotope envelope checks, in-source fragments ([NeoNexusX/SpatialXomics](https://github.com/NeoNexusX/SpatialXomics/tree/HEAD/src/features/assistant/skills/adduct-isotope-analysis) · ★1)
- **adduct-isotope-confidence-rating**: Use when interpreting mummichog metabolite predictions from an untargeted LC/MS feature table: predicted compounds must be rated by the presence of expected ion forms (M+H[1+], M−H[−], adducts, C13 isotopes) before counting or reporting them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotope-confidence-rating))
- **adduct-isotope-derivative-enumeration**: Use when an untargeted LC/MS feature table (m/z, retention time, p-value) must be mapped to metabolites without MS/MS spectra, by enumerating isotopic and adduct forms (e.g. M+H[1+], M(C13)+H[1+], M+Na[1+], M−H[−]) from neutral compound formulas in a genome-scale metabolic model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotope-derivative-enumeration))
- **adduct-isotope-pattern-annotation**: Use when a processed peak list (post peak-picking/alignment, e.g. from XCMS or the DIMS SIM-stitch pipeline) needs metabolite annotations where adduct forms and isotopic abundance patterns across co-eluting/co-occurring peaks should be exploited rather than matching single masses alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotope-pattern-annotation))
- **adduct-isotope-two-tier-tree-alignment**: Use when a feature table from LC-MS untargeted metabolomics or isotope tracing must be organized into empirical compounds: pattern-matched feature pairs (e.g. 1.0034 Da 13C/12C, 22.9893 Da Na+) form a mass distance network that needs tree conversion and neutral mass inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotope-two-tier-tree-alignment))
- **adduct-isotopologue-assignment**: Use when an Asari-derived feature table must be grouped into empirical compounds (empCpds) prior to MS1 or MS2 annotation, so that isotopologues and adducts of the same metabolite are annotated as one unit. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotopologue-assignment))
- **adduct-level-annotation-counting**: Use when comparing coverage of two or more in-silico lipid fragmentation libraries and you need a per-lipid-class, per-adduct count matrix of library entries and annotated features to flag classes present in only one library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-level-annotation-counting))
- **adduct-mass-adjustment-calculation**: Use when you have a set of in silico-predicted compounds (with SMILES structures) and an experimental metabolomics peak list (m/z values), and you need to filter predictions to only those that could plausibly be detected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-adjustment-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-calculation**: Use when building an in-house RT/m/z metabolite library from mzML files of authentic standards and you need expected per-adduct m/z values to target EIC extraction before peak finding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-calculation))
- **adduct-mass-calculation-and-matching**: Use when when you have a list of target molecules with known molecular formulas and need to extract and validate their peaks from centroided mzML files, or when assessing whether detected peaks in a mass spectrometry run correspond to expected adducts of known metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-calculation-and-matching) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-calculation-from-smiles**: Use when when you have a metabolite SMILES structure and need to predict which adduct ions will appear in a mass spectrum acquired with a chemical derivatizing matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-calculation-from-smiles) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-mass-calculation-positive-mode**: Use when a list of significant ion m/z values (e.g. up/down-regulated ions from an rMSIKeyIon 'ions' output list) must be assigned putative metabolite identities in positive-ion LDI/MSI data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-calculation-positive-mode))
- **adduct-mass-correction**: Use when preparing raw tandem MS/MS spectra (from NIST20/23 SDF exports or MassSpecGym TSVs) for ICEBERG/SCARF training or evaluation, where peaks must be expressed as neutral fragment masses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-correction))
- **adduct-mass-difference-matching**: Use when you have computed a histogram of pairwise mass differences from MS imaging data and need to (1) identify which observed mass differences correspond to biologically relevant or chemically known adducts, or (2) rank the most frequently observed mass differences to discover dominant adduct. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-difference-matching) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-mass-difference-ranking**: Use when you have computed a histogram of mass differences from all pairwise mass comparisons in your MALDI-MS imaging dataset and need to prioritize which mass differences are most frequent and likely represent genuine molecular adducts (e.g., metabolite + matrix ions) rather than noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-difference-ranking) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-matching-and-clustering**: Use when after identifying statistically significant LC-MS features (e.g. via MB-VIP permutation testing) when you need to consolidate redundant measurements of the same metabolite arising from different ionisation adducts (e.g. [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M−H]−). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-matching-and-clustering) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-mass-offset-assignment**: Use when when you have an LC-MS feature table with m/z and retention time columns and need to identify which observed ions correspond to the same neutral compound under different ionization conditions and isotopic enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-offset-assignment) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-offset-parameterization**: Use when when processing LC-MS metabolomics feature tables where adduct annotation is absent or incomplete, and you need to identify which ionization adducts are present in your mass spectrometry data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-offset-parameterization) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-range-restriction**: Use when preparing a CCS training set from the McLean Unified CCS Compendium for [M-H]-, [M+H]+ or [M+Na]+ adducts and entries must be restricted to the mass ranges supported by DeepCCS, AllCCS or CCSbase. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-range-restriction))
- **adduct-mass-shift-calculation**: Use when when you have a list of observed m/z values from LC/MS feature detection and need to identify candidate metabolites by testing whether those m/z values correspond to known database compounds in specific ionization forms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-shift-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-shift-calculation**: Use when preparing an m/z annotation reference index from SDF-derived compound databases (ChEBI, HMDB, GNPS, LIPIDMAPS) where each neutral mass must be expanded into ion masses for the polarity and adduct set being searched. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-shift-calculation--cb65827eae8aa981))
- **adduct-mass-shift-calculation**: Use when a list of unannotated experimental m/z peaks (e.g. 200 peaks of 150-300 Da from an E. coli untargeted metabolomics dataset) must be matched against predicted compounds from a Pickaxe network expansion by enumerating adducted m/z values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-shift-calculation--a10ff33217213487))
- **adduct-mass-subtraction-from-fragment-peaks**: Use when an MS2 peak list must be annotated with subformulae of a candidate precursor formula to score formula/adduct candidates de novo — e.g. when building inputs for a formula transformer and fragmentation trees are too slow or unnecessary. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-subtraction-from-fragment-peaks))
- **adduct-mz-rt-conflict-detection**: Use when finalizing an in-house LC-MS metabolite standard library built with metScribeR and you must flag adduct pairs whose m/z and RT overlap within the LC-MS method's separation ability before CSV export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mz-rt-conflict-detection))
- **adduct-mz-variant-prediction**: Use when a neutral-mass compound database (e.g. HMDB, FooDB) must be converted into an m/z-searchable annotation resource, or when matching observed peak m/z values (with a known ppm error margin) against compounds whose adduct and isotope variants are not yet enumerated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mz-variant-prediction))
- **adduct-notation-normalization**: Use when merging spectral-library or feature-table records whose ion-identity annotations appear in inconsistent spellings (e.g. 'M + H', 'M + H +', '[M + H]+') and you need them aggregated as one ion identity before networking or counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-notation-normalization))
- **adduct-precursor-mass-calculation**: Use when a compound list supplies molecular formulas or SMILES strings (target/suspect entries) and precursor ion masses must be derived under a chosen adduct before peak extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-precursor-mass-calculation))
- **adduct-probability-feature-correlation-scoring**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics dataset carries multiple putative annotations per feature (CMM-generated annotation tables) and annotations must be prioritized for pathway analysis using adduct probability and feature-correlation evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-probability-feature-correlation-scoring))
- **adduct-probability-ranking**: Use when a RowMerger-merged annotation table contains multiple putative annotations per feature and a feature-intensity table is available, and the goal is to prioritize the most probable annotation per feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-probability-ranking))
- **adduct-redundancy-accounting**: Use when counting unique lipid ions, features, or lipid types from annotation workbooks produced by LipidMatch or comparable lipid identification software, where multiple adducts of the same lipid species appear across features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-redundancy-accounting))
- **adduct-reference-mass-matching**: Use when you have a binned mass-difference histogram from MALDI-MSI peak pairs and want to count occurrences of known adducts (Na+, K+, 13C, DHB or CHCA matrix adducts) within instrument mass tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-reference-mass-matching))
- **adduct-regex-pattern-matching**: Use when ingesting mass spectrometry spectra from heterogeneous databases or libraries where adduct annotations may be incomplete, incorrectly formatted, or inconsistent with the ionization mode. - **adduct-relationship-inference**: Use when a processed, annotated LC-MS feature table (tidymass massdataset) contains suspected in-source fragmentation products misannotated as genuine metabolites and no in-pipeline deconvolution exists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-relationship-inference))
- **adduct-rule-based-ion-annotation**: Use when you have CAMERA pseudo-spectra from LC/MS data in positive or negative ion mode and need to label peaks as isotope, adduct or fragment species and derive the quasi-molecular ion mass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-rule-based-ion-annotation))
- **adduct-rule-table-matching**: Use when grouping has produced CAMERA compound spectra from LC/MS peak lists (positive and/or negative ESI mode) and you need to assign ion-species annotations and propose accurate compound masses from adduct/isotope rules. - **adduct-series-mass-difference-validation**: Use when identify_adducts() output from a direct-injection plasma ionization (DBDI) MS dataset must be checked: candidate features should share a base analyte and differ by exact O-adduct deltas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-series-mass-difference-validation))
- **adduct-signature-identification-mass-spectrometry**: Use when you have statistically significant LC-MS features (e.g., filtered by p-value &lt; 0.01) from multi-assay metabolomics datasets and need to group features that represent the same metabolite ionized under different ESI conditions (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+NH4]+). - **adduct-species-annotation**: Use when you have an untargeted LC-MS metabolomics feature table (m/z per sample) plus MS2 spectra in .mgf or .mzML format and need to assign metabolite identities to peaks at MS1 and MS2 levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-species-annotation))
- **adduct-species-simulation**: Use when you need to stress-test an LC-HRMS feature alignment tool (e.g. MetMatch) against a known ground truth by injecting artificial adduct variants and retention-time perturbations into a reference feature list. - **adduct-specific-model-fine-tuning**: Use when when you have access to annotated MS/MS spectra from a specific ionization mode (e.g., negative ESI) or adduct class (e. - **adduct-stratified-model-evaluation**: Use when you need to rank a machine-learning collision cross section predictor (e.g. CCSP 2.0) against alternatives like CCSbase, AllCCS or DeepCCS using held-out test splits drawn from a measured CCS compendium stratified by adduct type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-stratified-model-evaluation))
- **adduct-type-assignment-positive-mode**: Use when you have an unknown MS/MS spectrum with a measured precursor m/z and want to determine which positive-mode adduct type ([M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+K]+, etc.) is most likely responsible for ionization. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-doubt-driven-development) · ★1)

Use when cutting a release, choosing a semantic version bump, tagging, or writing a changelog. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-git-workflow-and-versioning) · ★1)
- **addy-agent-skills-idea-refine**: Refines raw ideas into sharp, actionable concepts through structured divergent and convergent thinking. Use when an idea is still vague, when you need to stress-test assumptions before committing to a plan, or when you want to expand options before converging on one. Triggers on ideate , refine this idea , or stress-test my plan . ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-idea-refine) · ★1)
- **addy-agent-skills-incremental-implementation**: Delivers changes incrementally in thin, verifiable slices. Use when implementing any feature or change that touches more than one file, or when picking up the next task from a plan. Use when rolling a change out behind a feature flag, when you're about to write a large amount of code at once, or when a task feels too big to land in one step. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-incremental-implementation) · ★1)
- **addy-agent-skills-interview-me**: Extracts what the user actually wants instead of what they think they should want. Achieves this through one-question-at-a-time interview until ~95% confidence about the underlying intent. Use when an ask is underspecified ( build me X without for whom or why now ), when the user explicitly invokes ( interview me , grill me , are we sure? , stress-test my thinking ), or when you catch yourself silently filling in ambiguous requirements before any plan, spec, or code exists. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-interview-me) · ★1)
- **addy-agent-skills-observability-and-instrumentation**: Instruments code so production behavior is visible and diagnosable. Use when adding logging, metrics, tracing, or alerting. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-observability-and-instrumentation) · ★1)
- **addy-agent-skills-performance-optimization**: Optimizes application performance across frontend, backend, queries, and databases. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-performance-optimization) · ★1)
- **addy-agent-skills-planning-and-task-breakdown**: Breaks work into ordered tasks. Use when you have a spec or clear requirements and need to break work into implementable tasks. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-planning-and-task-breakdown) · ★1)

Use when building any feature that accepts untrusted data, manages user sessions, or interacts with third-party services. - **adhd-cognitive-operating-system-framework**: ADHD 认知机制的**主题框架 Skill**（不是任何一位研究者的视角，不模拟任何人的语气）。 基于 5 份调研底稿、40+ 条同行评议论文与机构声明，提炼 6 个领域共识框架、6 个流派的并列对照、 16 条未定论分歧、12 条「我们不知道」清单。用于在不诊断、不给用药建议的前提下，分析注意力／ 执行功能／延迟折扣／任务条件的机制争议，并标注每个主张的证据等级与所属学派。 当用户提到「ADHD 机制」「执行功能还是动机」「缺陷还是天赋」「延迟折扣」「双通路模型」 「neurodiversity 与 ADHD」时使用。 ⚠️ 健康领域：不评估任何人是否患有 ADHD（**包括说话者自己**），不给药物、剂量、停药、加药建议。 ([lixvanran/adhd-persona-skills](https://github.com/lixvanran/adhd-persona-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adhd-cognitive-operating-system-framework))
